- **gemini-analysis**: - Gemini는 폴백 단계에서만 호출한다. 조회 순서는 메모리 캐시 → community_products(DB) → 라쿠텐 → Gemini이며, 바코드가 한 번이라도 community_products에 들어가면 그 뒤로 같은 바코드에 Gemini를 다시 부르지 않는다. - 라쿠텐 조회 실패 시 사용자가 제품의 정보/성분 부분을 촬영하면, Gemini가 사진 속 보이는 텍스트를 전부 읽어 일본어 원문 그대로 구조화한다. - Gemini는 번역하지 않는다. 번역은 번역 API(Papago/DeepL)가 일→한으로 처리한다. - 컬럼은 추가하지 않는다. 공용 테이블 community_products의 기존 컬럼에만 저장한다. - 공용으로 저장·재사용되는 값은 식별 정보(이름·브랜드·가격·이미지)까지다. Gemi ([kopo-Visited/app-in-toss-Bbik](https://github.com/kopo-Visited/app-in-toss-Bbik/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gemini-analysis))

([skflx/skflx.github.io](https://github.com/skflx/skflx.github.io/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gemini-delegate) · ★1)
- **gemini-delegate**: Save Claude Code token usage by delegating high-context tasks to Gemini CLI. Two mechanisms: (1) write-directly — Gemini generates/modifies files with --yolo, code never enters Claude's context; (2) structured handoff — Gemini returns a targeted file list or checklist, Claude acts without re-reading anything. AUTO-INVOKE for: tasks requiring more than 3-4 file reads; generating more than ~100 lines of code; whole-codebase analysis or CLAUDE.md generation; images (PNG, JPG, GIF, WEBP, SVG, BMP), video (MP4, MOV, AVI, WEBM), or PDF files; real-time web search or current information; large diff/… ([charlienew/gemini-delegate](https://github.com/charlienew/gemini-delegate/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **gemini-delegate**: Delegate an edit/implementation task (run) or a research/analysis/report task (report) to Gemini through Google's Antigravity CLI (agy), on an isolated copy of the project — no git required. Default model: Gemini 4 Hydrogen high (best tested option for web research and well-specified code); Gemini 4 Neon high for terminal/agentic and harder reasoning. Covers which Gemini model and effort level to pick, parallel runs, diff review and merge, quota, and the security limits of agy. Never against a production or live system. ([Daniboy2116/gemini-delegate](https://github.com/Daniboy2116/gemini-delegate/tree/HEAD/plugins/gemini-delegate/skills/gemini-delegate))
- **gemini-delegate**: Delegate work to the Google Gemini CLI — either as an implementer (worktree + yolo) or a read-only consultant (plan mode). Use when (1) the agent has failed 2+ attempts on the same task, (2) the user explicitly says "delegate to gemini", "ask gemini", "gemini review", "gemini fix", "let gemini handle it", (3) a second opinion from a different model is wanted on a plan, implementation, or design, (4) after a non-trivial plan is created and review is needed before coding. The agent auto-selects implementer vs consultant mode from the task; if ambiguous, asks the user. Same shape as a Codex-base… ([doruktarhan/doruk-ai-harness](https://github.com/doruktarhan/doruk-ai-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/delegation/gemini-delegate))
- **gemini-delegate**: Use when Codex should consult the local Gemini CLI for a second opinion, adversarial critique, architecture review, diff review, or a persistent side session in tmux. Trigger this skill when the user asks to talk to Gemini directly, wants model-diversity feedback, wants bounded overflow work routed to Gemini, or wants an isolated Gemini worker in a separate git worktree. Default to read-only consult mode; use the worker session only when Gemini must edit code outside Codex's active worktree. ([LiamVDB1/codex-config](https://github.com/LiamVDB1/codex-config/tree/HEAD/skills/gemini-delegate) · 2 copies)
- **gemini-delegate**: Delegate tasks to Gemini CLI for large context ingestion (2M+ tokens), screenshot/video processing, web browsing, browser automation, and bulk refactoring. Track token savings. ([PiotrMieszczak/ai_catalog](https://github.com/PiotrMieszczak/ai_catalog/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gemini-delegate))
- **gemini-delegate**: Hand a task to Gemini CLI when its 2M-token context or multimodal input is a real advantage, and report the tokens saved. Use when analysing an entire repository or very large file set, processing a screenshot or video, fact-checking current docs on the web, driving a browser, or making a high-volume low-complexity refactor. Not for reasoning, planning, or ordinary edits. ([PiotrMieszczak/llm_utils](https://github.com/PiotrMieszczak/llm_utils/tree/HEAD/archived/delegation/skills/gemini-delegate))
- **gemini-delegate**: Delegate bounded work to the locally installed Google Gemini CLI, including model selection/probing, thinking control, session continuity, cache-aware repeated PDF/multimodal review/writing, workspace-scoped coding, auth-aware Headless execution, and explicit Skill health/update/rollback maintenance. Use only when the user or parent agent explicitly asks for Gemini/Gemini CLI/Antigravity CLI (agy), asks about Gemini models/thinking, or asks to continue/resume a prior Gemini delegation. Do not activate for generic writing, review, analysis, or coding that does not request Gemini. ([techdou/gemini-delegate](https://github.com/techdou/gemini-delegate/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **gemini-delegate**: Delega tarefas de TEXTO com muito input e pouco output ao Gemini CLI, retornando só o resultado compacto ao contexto do Claude para economizar tokens. Use ao resumir/extrair de texto ou arquivos de texto longos (&gt;5k tokens), ao classificar lotes (&gt;20 itens) com critérios fixos, ou em qualquer padrão "ler muito, devolver pouco". Não use para imagens/PDF (o CLI não tem visão), geração de código ou conversa iterativa. ([www4lker/skills-walker](https://github.com/www4lker/skills-walker/tree/HEAD/skills/ferramentas/gemini-delegate))
- **gemini-delegation**: Delegates to the Gemini CLI. Use when delegation-core selects Gemini or 1M+ token context is needed. ([athola/claude-night-market](https://github.com/athola/claude-night-market/tree/HEAD/plugins/conjure/skills/gemini-delegation) · ★350)
- **gemini-delegation**: Delegate well-specified, test-verifiable coding work to Gemini 3.8 Flash through the agy-foreman MCP tools (agy_start, agy_get, agy_act, agy_status), and review the verified result. Use when executing implementation plans, bulk mechanical changes or large-codebase exploration where a machine check exists. Do not use for design decisions, small edits or tasks without tests. ([mountaser/agy-foreman](https://github.com/mountaser/agy-foreman/tree/HEAD/skills/gemini-delegation) · ★7 · 2 copies)
